=== CLASSIFICATION RULES ===
KEEP: canonical source files used by runtime/build.
COPY_VPS_TO_LOCAL: active VPS-only canonical runtime files.
RECONCILE: active file exists both sides but SHA256 differs.
ARCHIVE: historical milestone installers, patches, probes, evidence.
DELETE_AFTER_VERIFY: exact duplicates/temp/generated artifacts only after manifest + rollback snapshot.
DO_NOT_TOUCH_YET: unknown files, active runtime refs, payment/pricing code, data stores, secrets.
